Better: Use direct formula for delta of counts for swaps. Since C_s = ∑ h_i p_{i+s} etc. The corr full vector d[k] = full correlation. Under swap (a from1 to0, b from0 to1), change for correlation with p=1-h:
For each term ∑ h_i p_{i+k} for k? A term involving positions i and i+k. In binary, term is 1 iff two bits differ with one orientation. Let's index corruption vector c full length 2n-1. For shift k (where c[k]=(n-1+k)=∑ h_i p_{i+k}), term at (i,j) with j=i+k. Since p_j=1-h_j, term=1 if h_i=1,h_j=0. So ordered pair (i,j) direction from 1 to 0 at offset k=j-i. Thus corr counts directed ordered pairs (i,j) such that h_i=1, h_j=0. That's simpler! For binary, each ordered pair (i,j), i≠j, if 1->0 contributes to shift j-i. (i=j cannot because h_i(1-h_i)=0.) Good. Continuous terms fractional but binary exact. Then full vector c is histogram of ordered 1->0 pairs by difference.

Swap: a:1->0, b:0->1. Directed pairs involving a,b:
- Pairs (a,j) for j≠a, with a=1 originally contribute diff j-a. After a=0, no. Delta -1 at d=j-a for all j that were 1? Wait if j=0 and a=1, pair a->j zero becomes? If a becomes 0, terms with a as source disappear: for any j where h_j=0 originally, original contributed 1 at d=j-a. If h_j=1 originally, 0. So delta_source = - (1-h_j) at d=j-a.
- Pairs (i,a) with a as target: contributed if h_i=1 and h_a=0? original h_a=1 so no; after target 0 and source i=1 -> contributes 1. Delta_target = + h_i at d=a-i.

- from O to b: d=b-o

- from b to Z: d=z-b
- from O to a: d=a-o

Thus delta = (new contributions) - (old):
+ for d=a-o for o∈O
+ for d=a-b (b to a)
+ for d=z-b for z∈Z
- for d=z-a for z∈Z
- for d=b-o for o∈O
Where O = ones\{a,b}, Z=zeros\{a,b}. This is O(n). We can compute vector by scattering increments.

Given h, p=1-h. c(k) = ∑_{i=L}^{U} h_{i+k} p_i, where L=max(0,-k), U=min(n-1, n-1-k). Derivative w.r.t h_m:
For each valid i:
- term h_{i+k}: derivative +1 if m = i+k
- term -h_{i+k} h_i: derivative -h_i if m = i+k; -h_{i+k} if m = i.
So total:
For m index:
Contributions from i such that i+k=m (i=m-k): if i valid -> +(1 - h_i) = 1 - h_{m-k}
Contributions from i such that i=m: if m valid -> -h_{m+k}
Valid i each. So gradient for shift k:
Initialize g=0 length n.
L=max(0,-k), U=min(n-1,n-1-k).
If k >=0:
  source i range 0..n-1-k.
  Term +1 (1-h_i) at positions i+k (d..n-1) => g[d:n] += 1 - h[0:n-d]
  Term -h_{i+k} at positions i (0..n-d-1) => g[0:n-d] -= h[d:n]
If k<0 (d=-k, i range d..n-1, i+k=i-d from 0..n-d-1):
  + (1-h_i) at positions i-d (0..n-d-1) => g[0:n-d] += 1 - h[d:n]
  -h_{i-d} at positions i (d..n-1) => g[d:n] -= h[0:n-d]
This matches earlier. Need be careful signs: Q = ∑ h_{i+k} (1 - h_i) = ∑ h_{i+k} - h_{i+k}h_i. Derivative: first +1 at i+k; second -h_i at i+k, -h_{i+k} at i. Thus +1-h_i at i+k, -h_{i+k} at i. Good.
For k≥0: i=0..n-1-d, i+k=d..n-1. +1-h[0:n-d] to g[d:n]. -h[d:n] to g[0:n-d].
For k<0 d=-k: i=d..n-1, i+k=i-d=0..n-d-1. +1-h[d:n] to g[0:n-d]. -h[0:n-d] to g[d:n]. Good.

Let's design FFT correlation robust:
def corr_full_fft(a):
  n=len(a); N=1; while N<2*n-1: N*=2
  A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); B=np.fft.rfft(1.0-a,N)
  c=np.fft.irfft(A*np.conj(B), N)[:2*n-1]
  return c

Method:
- tmp = c_base.copy()
- Need update for i from old_i to new_i, using h values with j old (for neighbor if j). For each neighbor k ≠ i:
  d=abs(k-i), h_k = h_old[k] (since j old unless k=j? For first, j old; yes h_old works for all k).
  dir = 1 if k>i else -1.
  If k>i:

    c_minus[d] += (1-h_k)*(new_i-old_i)
  If k<i:
    c_plus[d] += (1-h_k)*delta
    c_minus[d] += -h_k*delta
  c0_i += new_i(1-new_i)-old_i(1-old_i)
- Then update for j from old_j to new_j, using current h: i new, others old. For each neighbor k ≠ j:
  h_k = new_i if k==i else old_h[k]
  delta = new_j-old_j
  If k>j:
    c_plus[d] += -h_k*delta
    c_minus[d] += (1-h_k)*delta
  If k<j:
    c_plus[d] += (1-h_k)*delta
    c_minus[d] += -h_k*delta
  c0_j += new_j(1-new_j)-old_j(1-old_j)

Let's implement pair line search carefully and efficiently.

Function `pair_search_step(h, c, i, j, n, grid_pts, rng)`:
Input h float, c float current, i,j distinct. Return t, best_val, lc, qc? To avoid building arrays if no interval.
Pseudo:
```
def pair_line_minimum(h, c, i, j, n, grid_pts=33):
    L = 2*n-1
    a = h[i]; b = h[j]
    # t such that x=a+t, y=b-t in [0,1]
    lo = max(-a, b-1.0)
    hi = min(1.0-a, b)
    if hi - lo < 1e-8: return None
    lc = np.zeros(L); qc = np.zeros(L)
    # pass i s=+1
    dv = 1.0 # coefficient for t
    # c0
    idx0 = n-1
    lc[idx0] += (1.0 - 2.0*a)  # s=+1 linear
    qc[idx0] -= 1.0
    # neighbors of i:
    # Need loop d=1..n-1:
    # right: idx_plus = n-1+d, idx_minus = n-1-d
    # left:
    for d in range(1, n-i):
        u = i+d; hu = h[u] # c0=hu,c1=0
        # u>i:
        lc[n-1+d] += -hu      # * t
        lc[n-1-d] += 1.0 - hu
    for d in range(1, i+1):
        u = i-d; hu = h[u]
        lc[n-1+d] += 1.0 - hu
        lc[n-1-d] += -hu
    # pass j s=-1, dv=-1, but neighbor i if adjacent? actually if u==i use h_i_new = a+t (c0=a,c1=1)
    b0=b
    # c0 j:
    lc[idx0] += -(1.0 - 2.0*b)  # s=-1 => -(1-2b)
    qc[idx0] -= 1.0
    # neighbors of j:
    for d in range(1, n-j): # right
        u=j+d
        if u == i:
           c0=a; c1=1.0
        else:
           c0=h[u]; c1=0.0
        # u>j, dv=-1:
        # plus: -h_u * dv = - (c0+c1 t)(-t)= (c0+c1 t)t => linear c0, quad c1*1? Wait -h_u*dv = -h_u*(-t)=h_u t = c0 t + c1 t^2. So lc += c0, qc += c1.
        lc[n-1+d] += c0
        qc[n-1+d] += c1
        # minus: (1-h_u)*dv = (1-c0-c1t)(-t) = -(1-c0)t + c1 t^2. linear = c0-1, quad=c1.
        lc[n-1-d] += c0 - 1.0
        qc[n-1-d] += c1
    for d in range(1, j+1): # left
        u=j-d
        if u == i:
           c0=a; c1=1.0
        else:
           c0=h[u]; c1=0.0
        # u<j, dv=-1:
        # plus: (1-h_u)*dv = (1-c0-c1t)(-t)= c0-1 linear + c1 quad
        lc[n-1+d] += c0 - 1.0
        qc[n-1+d] += c1
        # minus: -h_u*dv = -h_u*(-t)= c0 t + c1 t^2
        lc[n-1-d] += c0
        qc[n-1-d] += c1
    ts = np.linspace(lo, hi, grid_pts)
    ensure 0 in ts? If lo<0<hi, include 0 by replacing or appending. We don't want t=0? It's okay. For best, maybe 0.
    t2=ts*ts
    # max for each t
    # For memory: G*L, okay.
    vals = c[np.newaxis,:] + lc[np.newaxis,:] * ts[:,None] + qc[np.newaxis,:] * t2[:,None]
    f = np.max(vals, axis=1)
    best_pos = int(np.argmin(f)); best_t=ts[best_pos]; best_f=f[best_pos]
    # local refine around best_t:
    step = (hi-lo)/max(grid_pts,1)
    # Evaluate smaller grid of 21 points around best_t within [lo,hi]
    tcenter = best_t
    half = step*1.5
    fine = np.linspace(max(lo,tcenter-half), min(hi,tcenter+half), 21)
    tf2=fine*fine
    vals2 = c + lc*fine[:,None] + qc*tf2[:,None]
    f2 = np.max(vals2, axis=1)
    best_pos2 = int(np.argmin(f2)); best_t=fine[best_pos2]; best_f=f2[best_pos2]
    return best_t, best_f
```

def continuous_pair_optimize(h0, c0, n, time_limit, rng, pair_budget=80, grid=33, top_k=10, T0=0.25, Tend=0.02, verbose=False):
    h = h0.copy(); c = c0.copy()
    cur = float(c.max()); best = cur; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
    no_accept_count=0; total_pairs=0
    start=time.time()
    # maintain high/low index lists? update occasionally.
    high_thresh = np.quantile(h,0.75), low? We can update each sweep.
    while remaining:
       # if no acceptance many, maybe reset to best and jump? We'll keep current, but periodically if no accept for 500 pair evals, set h=best, c=best? Maybe to avoid drift. Or to current best to re-explore.
       # active indices
       top_idx = np.argsort(c)[-top_k:] if L <= 1000 else np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
       top_idx = top_idx[np.argsort(c[top_idx])[::-1]]
       cand_pairs = []
       seen = set()
       # active pairs:
       for idx in top_idx:
           k=idx-(n-1)
           Lstart=max(0,-k); Uend=min(n, n-k) # m range [L,U)
           if Uend<=Lstart: continue
           m_arr=np.arange(Lstart,Uend)
           left = h[m_arr]; right = h[m_arr+k]
           # contribution term if k? formula h[m+k](1-h[m]) always with vector right_left? If k can negative, m_arr+k might decrease but still in range; right? Use A = h[m_arr+k], B = h[m_arr]. term = A*(1-B)
           term = h[m_arr+k]*(1-h[m_arr])
           # Also opposite term? Since top idx is this orientation, term positive.
           # choose top terms where term > 0.1 maybe
           order = np.argsort(term)[::-1]
           take = 0
           for pos in order:
               if take>=8: break
               if term[pos] < 0.02: break
               p = int(m_arr[pos]); q = int(m_arr[pos]+k)
               if p==q: continue
               if p>q: p,q=q,p
               key=(p,q)
               if key in seen: continue
               seen.add(key)
               if abs(h[p]-h[q]) < 0.02: continue
               cand_pairs.append((p,q))
               take+=1
       # random high-low pairs:
       # choose high and low based on current h values
       # sample 30 random distinct pairs until candidates count enough
       for _ in range(30):
           if len(cand_pairs) >= pair_budget: break
           p = rng.randint(n); q=rng.randint(n)
           if p==q: continue
           if abs(h[p]-h[q]) <0.02: continue
           if p>q: p,q=q,p
           key=(p,q); if seen continue
           seen.add(key); cand_pairs.append((p,q))
       # limit maybe pair_budget
       cand_pairs = cand_pairs[:pair_budget]
       if not cand_pairs:
          cand_pairs=[(0,1)] if n>1 else []
       accepted=False
       for (p,q) in cand_pairs:
           if len(cand_pairs) large and remaining time per pair? check.
           res = pair_line_minimum(... grid)
           if res is None: continue
           t,bf,lc,qc=res
           # Maybe bf from coefficients; due drift compare with actual? if abs(tf)>0, update_estimate
           # Accept if bf < cur - 1e-10 or SA
           accept=False
           if bf < cur - 1e-10:
              accept=True
           else:
              # anneal: maybe if bf-cur <= T and rng < exp(-(bf-cur)/T)
              if bf - cur < 0.5:
                 if rng.rand() < math.exp(-(bf-cur)/T): accept=True
           if accept:
              if abs(t)>1e-12:
                 h[p] += t; h[q] -= t
                 # clip tiny
                 if h[p]<0: h[p]=0 ...
                 c += lc*t + qc*t*t
                 # fix negative tiny in c? c = np.maximum(c,0?) Correlation terms can be negative? h(1-h) nonnegative, sum nonnegative. Numerical may -1e-12. We can clip c to 0? But max unaffected if slightly negative. Clip c[c<0 & >-1e-9]=0. For correctness, maybe `np.maximum(c,0,out=c)`? But if true could be negative due error? true nonnegative. Use `np.clip(c, 0.0, None)`.
                 cur = float(c.max())
                 if cur < best - 1e-12:
                    best=cur; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
                 no_accept=0; accepted=True
              else:
                 pass
           # update T
       if not accepted:
          no_accept += pair_budget
          # if no_accept > some, rejuvenate: set h=best_h, c=best_c? Or random mutate
          if no_accept > 200:
              # take a random beneficial? We can choose best among random pairs even if worsens? or reset current to best and continue.
              h = best_h.copy(); c = best_c.copy(); cur=best; no_accept=0
       T decay
    return best_h, best, best_c

```
def project_feasible(h,n):
  h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  target=float(n)/2.0
  for _ in range(100):
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
    unsat=(h>1e-10)&(h<1-1e-10)
    if np.any(unsat):
       h[unsat]+=diff/np.sum(unsat)
    else:
       if diff>0:
           idx=np.argsort(h)[:?]
       else: ...
```
The previous simple adjust on all if saturated may break bounds. But saturated all means all 0 or1 and sum not target impossible if target integer? ```
if diff>0:
    # increase lowest values
    order=np.argsort(h)
else:
    order=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
# spread to maintain bounds
```
But if all saturated, to change sum by diff (maybe 0.5) need fractional. Increase lowest (0s) or decrease highest (1s). Do sequential add with clip.
```

   if diff>0:
      inc=min(diff,1-h[idx]); h[idx]+=inc; diff-=inc
   else:
      dec=min(-diff,h[idx]); h[idx]-=dec; diff+=dec
```

```
def apply_pair_move(h,c,i,j,t,lc,qc,n):
   a_old = h[i]; b_old = h[j]
   hp = a_old + t; hq = b_old - t
   # clamp with pair sum
   s = a_old + b_old
   if hp < 0: hp=0; elif hp>1: hp=1
   if hq <0: hq=0; elif hq>1: hq=1
   diff = s - hp - hq
   if diff != 0:
       if hp + diff <=1 and hp+diff>=0: hp += diff
       else: hq += diff
   h[i]=hp; h[j]=hq
   # use actual delta if not t? lc/qc assume t; if diff nonzero tiny, use t_actual=hp-a_old
   ta = hp - a_old
   c += lc*ta + qc*ta*ta
   # But h[j] maybe not exactly b_old - ta if diff on hq; tiny. Could update c with actual deltas for both? Coeffs assume exactly opposite. If diff tiny, okay.
   c[np.abs(c)<1e-12] = 0? maybe.
```

Implementation details for `pair_line_best`:
```
def pair_line_best(h, c, i, j, n, grid_pts=45):
    if i == j: return None
    if i > j: i,j = j,i? But if caller normalized, no. Do inside.
    a = float(h[i]); b = float(h[j])
    lo = max(-a, b - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0 - a, b)
    if not (hi > lo + 1e-9): return None
    L = 2*n - 1
    plus_lin = np.zeros(n-1, dtype=float)
    ...
```

`idx0=n-1`.
After coeff arrays, add to lc/qc:
```
lc = np.zeros(L)
qc = np.zeros(L)
lc[idx0] += (1.0 - 2.0*a) - (1.0 - 2.0*b)
qc[idx0] -= 2.0
if n > 1:
    dind = np.arange(1, n)
    pos_idx = idx0 + dind
    neg_idx = idx0 - dind
    lc[pos_idx] += plus_lin
    lc[neg_idx] += minus_lin
    qc[pos_idx] += plus_quad
    qc[neg_idx] += minus_quad
```

Generate ts:
```
if grid_pts < 2: grid_pts=2
ts = np.linspace(lo, hi, grid_pts)
# Ensure endpoints exactly and 0
if lo < 0.0 < hi:
    ts = np.concatenate((ts, [0.0]))
    ts = np.unique(ts)
# If lo negative but close to 0? unique sorted. We need not sorted.
t2 = ts*ts
# To reduce memory, if len(ts)*L > 200000 (n large, grid large) chunk? For n=512, 45*1023=46k, okay. No chunk. 
vals = c[np.newaxis, :] + lc[np.newaxis, :] * ts[:, None] + qc[np.newaxis, :] * t2[:, None]
f = np.max(vals, axis=1)
bp = int(np.argmin(f)); bt=ts[bp]; bf=float(f[bp])
# refine
step = (hi-lo)/max(grid_pts,1)
center=bt
half=step*2.0
flo=max(lo, center-half); fhi=min(hi, center+half)
fine = np.linspace(flo, fhi, 25)
if lo < 0.0 < hi:
   fine = np.unique(np.concatenate((fine, [0.0])))
t2f=fine*fine
vals2 = c[np.newaxis,:] + lc[np.newaxis,:]*fine[:,None] + qc[np.newaxis,:]*t2f[:,None]
f2 = np.max(vals2, axis=1)
bp2=int(np.argmin(f2)); bt2=fine[bp2]; bf2=float(f2[bp2])
if bf2 < bf:
   bt=bt2; bf=bf2
# Also include exact endpoints and 0 if not? unique did.

- If n odd, return initial? But for binary need even. Ensure h_initial balanced binary float.

- `c = binary_corr(h); cur = int(c.max()); best = cur; best_h=h.copy()`
- Maintain ones/zeros lists.
- Loop until deadline:
   - candidate pairs from active c top terms (term==1). For each top idx, get positions of contributing terms; sample `per_lag=min(12, len)`.
   - for each candidate (a,b), evaluate swap:
        ensure h[a]==1,h[b]==0 or vice versa? Active term code uses specific orientation but swap symmetric; if bits differ, swap. For binary, a must 1,b0 to maintain count. If active term h[b]=1,h[a]=0 (a=m,b=m+k), yes a zero,b one; swap. If for negative k, pair maybe a=m+k? Our candidate from term `h[m+k]&~h[m]`: source one at m+k, zero at m. Let p=m+ k (one), q=m (zero). Swap p<->q. Good. If bits differ but not exactly, check.
        Evaluate `cnew = binary_corr(h)` after swap; newm=max. Accept if newm < cur - 1e-12 or SA. For SA, `delta=newm-cur` integer; if delta <=0 accept; if delta==1 and rng<exp(-1/T) maybe; delta>1 reject.
        If accept, update h,c,cur, lists; if cur<best update.
   - random swaps: choose from ones_list, zeros_list; same evaluate. Limit e.g. 50 per iteration.
   - If no accept in an iteration, no_progress += number; if no_progress > 200, reset h=best_h,c=binary_corr(best_h),cur=best, no_progress=0, maybe T = 0.5.
   - T decay based on moves tried: `T = max(Tend, T0 * (Tend/T0)**(min(tried,5000)/5000))`.
- At end, return best_h float, c5=best*2/n.

Let's refine `project_feasible` exact sum to avoid total drift. Use if diff large sequential. For final, if h has sum exactly but tiny, okay. Code:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(3):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       unsat=(h>1e-10)&(h<1.0-1e-10)
       cnt=np.sum(unsat)
       if cnt>0:
          h[unsat] += diff/cnt
          h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
       else:
          if diff>0: order=np.argsort(h)
          else: order=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
          for idx in order:
             if diff>0:
                inc=min(diff,1.0-h[idx])
                if inc<=0: continue
                h[idx]+=inc; diff-=inc
             else:
                dec=min(-diff,h[idx])
                if dec<=0: continue
                h[idx]-=dec; diff+=dec
             if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
    # final exact if tiny
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       # adjust unsat one with smallest distance to bound
       if diff>0: idx=np.argmin(1.0-h)
       else: idx=np.argmin(h)
       h[idx]+=diff
       h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
       # if still not exact due clip, distribute again
    return h
```
If n odd target .5, values maybe. Good.

`threshold_balance(v,n)`: For n even. If n odd, project continuous? But binary not. We'll only call even.
```
def threshold_balance(v,n):
    b=(v>0.5).astype(float)
    ones=int(b.sum()); target=n//2
    if ones>target:
       idx=np.where(b==1.0)[0]
       order=idx[np.argsort(v[idx])]  # lowest v first
       b[order[:ones-target]]=0.0
    elif ones<target:
       idx=np.where(b==0.0)[0]
       order=idx[np.argsort(-v[idx])] # highest v first
       b[order[:target-ones]]=1.0
    return b
```
If v all 0/1 and ones=target, okay. If n even.

Let's write continuous_pair_optimize pseudocode more concretely:
```
def continuous_pair_optimize(h0,c0,n,deadline,rng, max_pairs=100, top_k=14, per_lag=8, grid_pts=45, T0=0.1, Tend=0.01):
    h = h0.copy(); c = c0.copy(); np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
    cur = float(c.max()); best = cur; best_h = h.copy(); best_c = c.copy()
    moves = 0; tried = 0; stale = 0; accept_iter = 0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        # active candidates
        L=len(c)
        topk = min(top_k,L)
        top_idx = np.argsort(c)[-topk:]
        top_idx = top_idx[np.argsort(c[top_idx])[::-1]]
        cands=[]; seen=set()
        for idx in top_idx:
            k=idx-(n-1)
            ls=max(0,-k); ue=min(n,n-k)
            if ue<=ls: continue
            m=np.arange(ls,ue)
            term = h[m+k]*(1.0-h[m])
            # maybe term could be negative? h in [0,1], nonneg.
            if term.size ==0: continue
            if term.max() < 0.005: continue
            order = np.argsort(term)[::-1]
            cnt=0
            for pos in order:
                if cnt >= per_lag or term[pos] < 0.005: break
                p = int(m[pos]); q = int(m[pos]+k)
                if p==q: continue
                if p>q: p,q=q,p
                if abs(h[p]-h[q]) < 0.01: continue
                key=(p,q)
                if key not in seen:
                   seen.add(key); cands.append(key); cnt+=1
        # random high-low pairs
        rnd_count = max(0, max_pairs - len(cands))
        for _ in range(min(rnd_count, 30)):
            p=rng.randint(n); q=rng.randint(n)
            if p==q: continue
            if abs(h[p]-h[q]) < 0.02: continue
            if p>q: p,q=q,p
            key=(p,q)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key); cands.append(key)
        if not cands:
             cands=[(0,1)] if n>1 else []
        cands=cands[:max_pairs]
        improved_this_iter=False
        for p,q in cands:
            if time.time() >= deadline: break
            tried += 1
            res = pair_line_best(h,c,p,q,n,grid_pts)
            if res is None: continue
            t,bf,lc,qc,lo,hi = res
            if abs(t) < 1e-9: continue
            if bf < cur - 1e-11:
                apply_pair_move(h,c,p,q,t,lc,qc)
                cur = float(c.max())
                improved_this_iter=True
                moves += 1
                if cur < best - 1e-12:
                    best = cur; best_h = h.copy(); best_c = c.copy(); stale=0
                if moves % 100 == 0:
                    # sync if h out or every few
                    if h.min() < -1e-8 or h.max() > 1.0+1e-8:
                        h = np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
                    c = correlation_full(h); np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
                    cur = float(c.max())
                    if cur < best - 1e-12:
                        best=cur; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy()
        if improved_this_iter:
            stale=0
        else:
            stale += 1
            # random perturbations
            if time.time() < deadline:
                T = max(Tend, T0 * math.exp(-tried/5000.0)) # or linear
                for _ in range(20):
                    if time.time() >= deadline: break
                    p=rng.randint(n); q=rng.randint(n)
                    if p==q: continue
                    if p>q: p,q=q,p
                    build = build_pair_coeffs(h,p,q,n)
                    if build is None: continue
                    lc,qc,lo,hi=build
                    # choose t
                    if hi-lo < 1e-8: continue
                    r = rng.rand()
                    if r < 0.4:
                        t=rng.uniform(lo,hi)
                    else:
                        t=rng.uniform(-0.05,0.05)
                        if t<lo: t=lo+rng.rand()*(hi-lo)*0.1
                        elif t>hi: t=hi-rng.rand()*(hi-lo)*0.1
                    if abs(t)<1e-9: continue
                    new_c = c + lc*t + qc*t*t
                    # maybe clip small negative
                    newmax = float(np.max(new_c))
                    delta = newmax - cur
                    accepted = False
                    if delta < -1e-11:
                        accepted = True
                    elif delta < 2*T and T > 1e-6:
                        if rng.rand() < math.exp(-delta/T):
                            accepted = True
                    if accepted:
                        h[p] += t; h[q] -= t
                        c[:] = new_c
                        np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
                        cur = newmax
                        tried +=1
                        if cur < best - 1e-12:
                            best=cur; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); stale=0
                        # If worsened too much, maybe revert? We'll allow but check
                        if cur > best + 0.3:
                            # revert by restoring from best? We don't have old c. Easiest reset current to best now.
                            h = best_h.copy(); c = best_c.copy(); cur = best; break
        if stale > 15:
            # reset to best, maybe increase T? We don't have param; use T0? stale reset
            h = best_h.copy(); c = best_c.copy(); cur = best; stale = 0
        if tried > 20000: break? no.
```

import numpy as np
import time
import math

def correlation_full(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')

def c5_from_c(c,n):
    return float(np.max(c)) * (2.0 / n)

def project_feasible(h,n): ...

def make_golden(n): ...
def make_vdc(n): ...

    p = 1 - h
    return np.correlate(h, p, mode='full')

### Code details

```
def build_pair_coeffs(h, i, j, n):
    if i == j: return None
    if i > j: i,j = j,i
    a = float(h[i]); b = float(h[j])
    lo = max(-a, b - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0 - a, b)
    if not (hi > lo + 1e-10): return None
    L = 2*n - 1
    if n == 1:
       lc=np.zeros(L); qc=np.zeros(L); return lc,qc,lo,hi
    plus_lin = np.zeros(n-1)
    minus_lin = np.zeros(n-1)
    plus_quad = np.zeros(n-1)
    minus_quad = np.zeros(n-1)
    idx0 = n-1
    lc = np.zeros(L)
    qc = np.zeros(L)
    lc[idx0] += (1.0 - 2.0*a) - (1.0 - 2.0*b)
    qc[idx0] -= 2.0
    # pass i
    r = n - 1 - i
    if r > 0:
       hu = h[i+1:n]
       plus_lin[:r] -= hu
       minus_lin[:r] += 1.0 - hu
    l = i
    if l > 0:
       hu = h[i-1::-1]
       plus_lin[:l] += 1.0 - hu
       minus_lin[:l] -= hu
    # pass j
    r2 = n - 1 - j
    if r2 > 0:
       c0 = h[j+1:n].copy()
       c1 = np.zeros(r2)
       if i > j:
          pos = i - (j + 1)
          if 0 <= pos < r2:
             c0[pos] = a
             c1[pos] = 1.0
       plus_lin[:r2] += c0
       plus_quad[:r2] += c1
       minus_lin[:r2] += c0 - 1.0
       minus_quad[:r2] += c1
    l2 = j
    if l2 > 0:
       c0 = h[j-1::-1].copy()
       c1 = np.zeros(l2)
       if i < j:
          pos = (j - 1) - i
          if 0 <= pos < l2:
             c0[pos] = a
             c1[pos] = 1.0
       plus_lin[:l2] += c0 - 1.0
       plus_quad[:l2] += c1
       minus_lin[:l2] += c0
       minus_quad[:l2] += c1
    dind = np.arange(1, n)
    pos_idx = idx0 + dind
    neg_idx = idx0 - dind
    lc[pos_idx] += plus_lin
    lc[neg_idx] += minus_lin
    qc[pos_idx] += plus_quad
    qc[neg_idx] += minus_quad
    return lc, qc, lo, hi
```
Since always i<j after normalize, branch i>j not used but okay.
For pass j right, if i>j (not normalized), pos maybe; okay.
For pass j left, if i<j, pos. Good.

Potential issue: If normalized i<j, pass j right `i>j` false. pass j left c1 at i. Good.

```
def pair_line_best(h,c,i,j,n,grid_pts=45):
   build=build_pair_coeffs(...)
   if build is None: return None
   lc,qc,lo,hi=build
   m = max(3, int(grid_pts))
   ts=np.linspace(lo,hi,m)
   if lo < 0.0 < hi:
       ts=np.unique(np.concatenate((ts, [0.0])))
   t2=ts*ts
   # if len(ts)*len(c) too large? memory okay
   vals = c[np.newaxis, :] + lc[np.newaxis, :] * ts[:, None] + qc[np.newaxis, :] * t2[:, None]
   f = np.max(vals, axis=1)
   bp = int(np.argmin(f)); bt=float(ts[bp]); bf=float(f[bp])
   step = (hi-lo)/float(max(m,1))
   flo = max(lo, bt - 2.0*step); fhi = min(hi, bt + 2.0*step)
   fine=np.linspace(flo,fhi,25)
   if lo < 0.0 < hi:
      fine=np.unique(np.concatenate((fine, [0.0])))
   t2=...
   f2=...
   ...
   return (bt,bf,lc,qc,lo,hi)
```
Use `np.unique` sorts; good. If hi-lo very small, fine endpoints maybe same; np.linspace handles.
If `len(fine)==0`? no.
If c has length L; if L=1 (n=1), dind empty; but n>1. Good.

```
def apply_pair_move(h,c,i,j,t,lc,qc):
    if abs(t)<1e-13: return
    h[i] += t
    h[j] -= t
    c += lc*t + qc*t*t
    # Clean tiny negatives only if cheap
    # c may be int? If c from binary int? But continuous_pair c float. If c int16? continuous uses float. OK.
    # Could do if np.any(c<0) but checks full. Do:
    if c.size < 5000:
       np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
```

`make_vdc`: The reversed bit method maybe produces sequence. Let's implement simpler:
```
def make_vdc(n):
    h=np.zeros(n)
    for i in range(n):
        val=i+1; rev=0; mask=1
        for _ in range(20):
            if val & 1: rev |= mask
            mask <<=1; val >>=1
        h[i]=1.0 if (rev & 1)==0 else 0.0
    return threshold_balance(h,n)
```
This maps VDC parity? It's okay.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def correlation_full(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = float(n) / 2.0

    for _ in range(3):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        unsat = (h > 1e-10) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-10)
        cnt = int(np.sum(unsat))
        if cnt > 0:
            h[unsat] += diff / float(cnt)
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            if diff > 0.0:
                order = np.argsort(h)
            else:
                order = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
            for idx in order:
                if diff > 0.0:
                    inc = min(diff, 1.0 - float(h[idx]))
                    if inc <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    h[idx] += inc
                    diff -= inc
                else:
                    dec = min(-diff, float(h[idx]))
                    if dec <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    h[idx] -= dec
                    diff += dec
                if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
                    break

    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmin(1.0 - h))
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmin(h))
        h[idx] += diff
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def interp_to(src, src_n, dst_n):
    if src_n == dst_n:
        return np.array(src, dtype=float).copy()
    xp = (np.arange(src_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(src_n))
    xn = (np.arange(dst_n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / float(dst_n))
    return np.interp(xn, xp, np.asarray(src, dtype=float))


def threshold_balance(v, n):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    b = (v > 0.5).astype(float)
    ones = int(np.sum(b))
    target = n // 2
    if ones > target:
        idx = np.where(b == 1.0)[0]
        if idx.size > 0:
            order = idx[np.argsort(v[idx])]
            b[order[: ones - target]] = 0.0
    elif ones < target:
        idx = np.where(b == 0.0)[0]
        if idx.size > 0:
            order = idx[np.argsort(-v[idx])]
            b[order[: target - ones]] = 1.0
    return b


def random_binary(n, rng):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n > 0:
        pos = rng.choice(n, size=n // 2, replace=False)
        h[pos] = 1.0
    return h


def make_thue(n):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        if bin(i).count("1") % 2 == 1:
            h[i] = 1.0
    if n % 2 == 0:
        h = threshold_balance(h, n)
    return h


def make_golden(n):
    gr = (1.0 + math.sqrt(5.0)) / 2.0
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        frac = (i * gr) % 1.0
        if abs(frac - 0.5) < 0.25:
            h[i] = 1.0
    if n % 2 == 0:
        h = threshold_balance(h, n)
    return h


def make_vdc(n):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for i in range(n):
        val = i + 1
        rev = 0
        mask = 1
        for _ in range(20):
            if val & 1:
                rev |= mask
            mask <<= 1
            val >>= 1
        if (rev & 1) == 0:
            h[i] = 1.0
    if n % 2 == 0:
        h = threshold_balance(h, n)
    return h


def binary_corr(h):
    p = 1 - h
    return np.correlate(h, p, mode="full")


def improve_binary(h_initial, n, deadline, rng, max_pairs=110, top_k=12, per_lag=12,
                   T0=1.0, Tend=0.05):
    if n % 2 != 0 or n <= 2:
        h = project_feasible(h_initial, n)
        c = correlation_full(h)
        return h, float(np.max(c)) * (2.0 / float(n))

    h = threshold_balance(h_initial, n).astype(np.int16)
    c = binary_corr(h)
    cur = int(np.max(c))
    best = cur
    best_h = h.copy()
    ones_list = list(np.where(h == 1)[0])
    zeros_list = list(np.where(h == 0)[0])

    tried = 0
    stale = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        top_idx = np.argsort(c)[-top_k:]
        top_idx = top_idx[np.argsort(c[top_idx])[::-1]]

        cands = []
        seen = set()
        for idx in top_idx:
            k = int(idx) - (n - 1)
            ls = max(0, -k)
            ue = min(n, n - k)
            if ue <= ls:
                continue
            m = np.arange(ls, ue)
            term_mask = (h[m + k] == 1) & (h[m] == 0)
            pos = np.nonzero(term_mask)[0]
            if pos.size == 0:
                continue
            if pos.size > per_lag:
                pos = rng.choice(pos, size=per_lag, replace=False)
            for p in pos:
                a = int(m[p] + k)
                b = int(m[p])
                if a == b:
                    continue
                if h[a] != 1 or h[b] != 0:
                    continue
                key = (min(a, b), max(a, b))
                if key in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(key)
                cands.append((a, b))
                if len(cands) >= max_pairs:
                    break
            if len(cands) >= max_pairs:
                break

        advanced = False

        for a, b in cands:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            if h[a] != 1 or h[b] != 0:
                continue
            tried += 1
            h[a] = 0
            h[b] = 1
            cnew = binary_corr(h)
            newm = int(np.max(cnew))
            delta = newm - cur
            T = max(Tend, T0 * (Tend / T0) ** (min(float(tried), 12000.0) / 12000.0))
            accept = False
            if delta < 0:
                accept = True
            elif delta == 0 and rng.rand() < 0.25:
                accept = True
            elif delta == 1 and T > 1e-8 and rng.rand() < math.exp(-1.0 / T):
                accept = True

            if accept:
                c = cnew
                cur = newm
                advanced = True
                stale = 0
                ones_list = list(np.where(h == 1)[0])
                zeros_list = list(np.where(h == 0)[0])
                if cur < best:
                    best = cur
                    best_h = h.copy()
            else:
                h[a] = 1
                h[b] = 0
                stale += 1

        if len(ones_list) > 0 and len(zeros_list) > 0:
            rand_count = 35
            for _ in range(rand_count):
                if time.time() >= deadline:
                    break
                ia = rng.randint(len(ones_list))
                ib = rng.randint(len(zeros_list))
                a = ones_list[ia]
                b = zeros_list[ib]
                if a == b:
                    continue
                tried += 1
                h[a] = 0
                h[b] = 1
                cnew = binary_corr(h)
                newm = int(np.max(cnew))
                delta = newm - cur
                T = max(Tend, T0 * (Tend / T0) ** (min(float(tried), 12000.0) / 12000.0))
                accept = False
                if delta < 0:
                    accept = True
                elif delta == 0 and rng.rand() < 0.2:
                    accept = True
                elif delta == 1 and T > 1e-8 and rng.rand() < math.exp(-1.0 / T):
                    accept = True

                if accept:
                    c = cnew
                    cur = newm
                    advanced = True
                    stale = 0
                    ones_list = list(np.where(h == 1)[0])
                    zeros_list = list(np.where(h == 0)[0])
                    if cur < best:
                        best = cur
                        best_h = h.copy()
                else:
                    h[a] = 1
                    h[b] = 0
                    stale += 1

        if not advanced:
            stale += max_pairs
        if stale > 5000:
            h = best_h.copy()
            c = binary_corr(h)
            cur = int(np.max(c))
            ones_list = list(np.where(h == 1)[0])
            zeros_list = list(np.where(h == 0)[0])
            stale = 0

    return best_h.astype(float), float(best) * (2.0 / float(n))


def build_pair_coeffs(h, i, j, n):
    if i == j:
        return None
    if i > j:
        i, j = j, i
    a = float(h[i])
    b = float(h[j])
    lo = max(-a, b - 1.0)
    hi = min(1.0 - a, b)
    if not (hi > lo + 1e-10):
        return None

    L = 2 * n - 1
    idx0 = n - 1
    lc = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    qc = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    lc[idx0] += (1.0 - 2.0 * a) - (1.0 - 2.0 * b)
    qc[idx0] -= 2.0

    if n > 1:
        plus_lin = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=float)
        minus_lin = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=float)
        plus_quad = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=float)
        minus_quad = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=float)

        r = n - 1 - i
        if r > 0:
            hu = h[i + 1:n]
            plus_lin[:r] -= hu
            minus_lin[:r] += 1.0 - hu

        l = i
        if l > 0:
            hu = h[i - 1::-1]
            plus_lin[:l] += 1.0 - hu
            minus_lin[:l] -= hu

        r2 = n - 1 - j
        if r2 > 0:
            c0 = h[j + 1:n].copy()
            c1 = np.zeros(r2, dtype=float)
            if i > j:
                pos = i - (j + 1)
                if 0 <= pos < r2:
                    c0[pos] = a
                    c1[pos] = 1.0
            plus_lin[:r2] += c0
            plus_quad[:r2] += c1
            minus_lin[:r2] += c0 - 1.0
            minus_quad[:r2] += c1

        l2 = j
        if l2 > 0:
            c0 = h[j - 1::-1].copy()
            c1 = np.zeros(l2, dtype=float)
            if i < j:
                pos = (j - 1) - i
                if 0 <= pos < l2:
                    c0[pos] = a
                    c1[pos] = 1.0
            plus_lin[:l2] += c0 - 1.0
            plus_quad[:l2] += c1
            minus_lin[:l2] += c0
            minus_quad[:l2] += c1

        dind = np.arange(1, n)
        pos_idx = idx0 + dind
        neg_idx = idx0 - dind
        lc[pos_idx] += plus_lin
        lc[neg_idx] += minus_lin
        qc[pos_idx] += plus_quad
        qc[neg_idx] += minus_quad

    return lc, qc, float(lo), float(hi)


def pair_line_best(h, c, i, j, n, grid_pts=45):
    built = build_pair_coeffs(h, i, j, n)
    if built is None:
        return None
    lc, qc, lo, hi = built

    mpt = max(3, int(grid_pts))
    ts = np.linspace(lo, hi, mpt)
    if lo < 0.0 < hi:
        ts = np.unique(np.concatenate((ts, np.array([0.0], dtype=float))))

    t2 = ts * ts
    vals = c[np.newaxis, :] + lc[np.newaxis, :] * ts[:, np.newaxis] + qc[np.newaxis, :] * t2[:, np.newaxis]
    f = np.max(vals, axis=1)
    bp = int(np.argmin(f))
    bt = float(ts[bp])
    bf = float(f[bp])

    step = (hi - lo) / float(max(mpt, 1))
    flo = max(lo, bt - 2.0 * step)
    fhi = min(hi, bt + 2.0 * step)
    fine = np.linspace(flo, fhi, 25)
    if lo < 0.0 < hi:
        fine = np.unique(np.concatenate((fine, np.array([0.0], dtype=float))))
    tf = fine * fine
    vals2 = c[np.newaxis, :] + lc[np.newaxis, :] * fine[:, np.newaxis] + qc[np.newaxis, :] * tf[:, np.newaxis]
    f2 = np.max(vals2, axis=1)
    bp2 = int(np.argmin(f2))
    if float(f2[bp2]) < bf:
        bt = float(fine[bp2])
        bf = float(f2[bp2])

    return bt, bf, lc, qc, lo, hi


def apply_pair_move(h, c, i, j, t, lc, qc):
    if abs(t) < 1e-13:
        return
    h[i] += t
    h[j] -= t
    c += lc * t + qc * t * t


def continuous_pair_optimize(h0, c0, n, deadline, rng, max_pairs=100, top_k=14,
                             per_lag=8, grid_pts=45, T0=0.12, Tend=0.008):
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h0, dtype=float).copy(), n)
    c = correlation_full(h)
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    cur = float(np.max(c))
    best = cur
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()

    tried = 0
    stale = 0
    sync_counter = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        L = len(c)
        topk = min(top_k, L)
        top_idx = np.argsort(c)[-topk:]
        top_idx = top_idx[np.argsort(c[top_idx])[::-1]]

        cands = []
        seen = set()

        for idx in top_idx:
            k = int(idx) - (n - 1)
            ls = max(0, -k)
            ue = min(n, n - k)
            if ue <= ls:
                continue
            m = np.arange(ls, ue)
            term = h[m + k] * (1.0 - h[m])
            if term.size == 0 or float(np.max(term)) < 0.004:
                continue
            order = np.argsort(term)[::-1]
            cnt = 0
            for pos in order:
                if cnt >= per_lag or term[pos] < 0.004:
                    break
                p = int(m[pos])
                q = int(m[pos] + k)
                if p == q:
                    continue
                if p > q:
                    p, q = q, p
                if abs(float(h[p]) - float(h[q])) < 0.01:
                    continue
                key = (p, q)
                if key in seen:
                    continue
                seen.add(key)
                cands.append(key)
                cnt += 1

        need = max(0, int(max_pairs) - len(cands))
        ok_pairs = 0
        while ok_pairs < need and ok_pairs < 40:
            p = int(rng.randint(n))
            q = int(rng.randint(n))
            if p == q:
                ok_pairs += 1
                continue
            if abs(float(h[p]) - float(h[q])) < 0.02:
                ok_pairs += 1
                continue
            if p > q:
                p, q = q, p
            key = (p, q)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                cands.append(key)
            ok_pairs += 1

        if len(cands) == 0 and n > 1:
            cands.append((0, 1))
        cands = cands[:max_pairs]

        improved = False
        for p, q in cands:
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                break
            tried += 1
            res = pair_line_best(h, c, p, q, n, grid_pts=grid_pts)
            if res is None:
                continue
            t, bf, lc, qc, lo, hi = res
            if abs(t) < 1e-9:
                continue
            if bf < cur - 1e-11:
                apply_pair_move(h, c, p, q, t, lc, qc)
                sync_counter += 1
                if sync_counter % 160 == 0 and (float(h.min()) < -1e-8 or float(h.max()) > 1.0 + 1e-8):
                    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
                cur = float(np.max(c))
                improved = True
                if cur < best - 1e-12:
                    best = cur
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    best_c = c.copy()
                    stale = 0
                if sync_counter % 160 == 0:
                    h = project_feasible(h, n)
                    c = correlation_full(h)
                    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
                    cur = float(np.max(c))
                    if cur < best - 1e-12:
                        best = cur
                        best_h = h.copy()
                        best_c = c.copy()
                    sync_counter = 0

        if not improved:
            stale += 1
            T = max(Tend, T0 * (Tend / T0) ** (min(float(tried), 8000.0) / 8000.0))
            if stale >= 3 or T > 0.5 * Tend:
                for _ in range(22):
                    if time.time() >= deadline:
                        break
                    p = int(rng.randint(n))
                    q = int(rng.randint(n))
                    if p == q:
                        continue
                    if p > q:
                        p, q = q, p
                    built = build_pair_coeffs(h, p, q, n)
                    if built is None:
                        continue
                    lc, qc, lo, hi = built
                    if hi - lo < 1e-8:
                        continue
                    r = rng.rand()
                    if r < 0.45:
                        t = float(rng.uniform(lo, hi))
                    else:
                        t = float(rng.uniform(-0.05, 0.05))
                        if t < lo:
                            t = lo + rng.rand() * (hi - lo) * 0.1
                        elif t > hi:
                            t = hi - rng.rand() * (hi - lo) * 0.1
                    if abs(t) < 1e-9:
                        continue
                    new_c = c + lc * t + qc * t * t
                    np.maximum(new_c, 0.0, out=new_c)
                    newm = float(np.max(new_c))
                    delta = newm - cur
                    accept = False
                    if delta < -1e-11:
                        accept = True
                    elif delta < 2.0 * T and T > 1e-7:
                        if rng.rand() < math.exp(-max(0.0, delta) / T):
                            accept = True
                    if accept:
                        h[p] += t
                        h[q] -= t
                        c[:] = new_c
                        tried += 1
                        cur = newm
                        if cur < best - 1e-12:
                            best = cur
                            best_h = h.copy()
                            best_c = c.copy()
                            stale = 0
                        if delta < 0.0:
                            stale = 0
                        if cur > best + 0.55:
                            h = best_h.copy()
                            c = best_c.copy()
                            cur = best
                            break

        if stale > 25:
            h = best_h.copy()
            c = best_c.copy()
            cur = best
            stale = 0

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    c_out = correlation_full(best_h)
    count = float(np.max(c_out))
    c5 = count * (2.0 / float(n))
    return best_h, c5, count


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.time()
    budget_s = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = start + budget_s
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    n1 = 160
    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        pn = int(parent.size)
        if pn % 2 == 0 and 64 <= pn <= 400:
            n1 = pn
    if parent is not None and parent.size != n1:
        parent = interp_to(parent, parent.size, n1)

    best_h = None
    best_c5 = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    if parent is not None:
        h0 = project_feasible(parent, n1)
        c0 = correlation_full(h0)
        c50 = float(np.max(c0)) * (2.0 / float(n1))
        best_h = h0
        best_c5 = c50
        best_n = n1
    else:
        h0 = random_binary(n1, rng)
        h0 = project_feasible(h0, n1)
        c0 = correlation_full(h0)
        best_c5 = float(np.max(c0)) * (2.0 / float(n1))
        best_h = h0
        best_n = n1

    if budget_s <= 30.0 or time.time() + 5.0 >= deadline:
        best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
        out_c = correlation_full(best_h)
        final_c5 = float(np.max(out_c)) * (2.0 / float(best_n))
        try:
            ec = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
            if ec is not None and ec < final_c5:
                final_c5 = float(ec)
        except Exception:
            pass
        return best_h, final_c5, best_n

    binary_results = []
    if best_n % 2 == 0 and time.time() + 40.0 < deadline:
        bin_deadline = min(deadline - 160.0, time.time() + max(35.0, 0.18 * budget_s))
        bin_seeds = []
        if best_h is not None:
            bin_seeds.append(theme := threshold_balance(best_h, best_n))
        bin_seeds.append(make_thue(best_n))
        bin_seeds.append(make_golden(best_n))
        bin_seeds.append(make_vdc(best_n))
        bin_seeds.append(random_binary(best_n, rng))

        for idx, hb in enumerate(bin_seeds):
            if time.time() + 10.0 >= bin_deadline:
                break
            rem = bin_deadline - time.time()
            if rem <= 0.0:
                break
            left = max(1, len(bin_seeds) - idx)
            per_dl = min(bin_deadline, time.time() + max(10.0, rem / float(left)))
            try:
                bh, bc5 = improve_binary(hb, best_n, per_dl, rng)
            except Exception:
                continue
            binary_results.append((float(bc5), len(binary_results), bh))
            if bc5 < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                best_c5 = bc5
                best_h = bh
                best_n = best_n

    binary_results.sort()
    cont_seeds = []
    if parent is not None:
        cont_seeds.append(project_feasible(parent, best_n))
    for bc5, idx, bh in binary_results[:3]:
        cont_seeds.append(np.asarray(bh, dtype=float).copy())
    if len(cont_seeds) == 0:
        cont_seeds.append(make_thue(best_n))
    cont_seeds = cont_seeds[:4]

    reserve_multi = 0.0
    if budget_s > 900.0:
        reserve_multi = min(240.0, 0.15 * budget_s)
    reserve_final = 30.0
    cont_limit = deadline - reserve_final - reserve_multi
    if time.time() + 40.0 > cont_limit:
        cont_limit = deadline - reserve_final
    cont_limit = min(deadline - reserve_final, max(time.time() + 25.0, cont_limit))

    def params_for(nn):
        if nn <= 220:
            return dict(max_pairs=110, top_k=14, per_lag=8, grid_pts=45)
        if nn <= 340:
            return dict(max_pairs=80, top_k=12, per_lag=7, grid_pts=41)
        return dict(max_pairs=60, top_k=10, per_lag=6, grid_pts=33)

    for idx_seed, hseed in enumerate(cont_seeds):
        if time.time() + 20.0 >= cont_limit:
            break
        left = cont_limit - time.time()
        if left <= 0.0:
            break
        if idx_seed == 0:
            slice_len = 0.55 * left
        else:
            rer = max(1, len(cont_seeds) - idx_seed)
            slice_len = left / float(rer)
        slice_len = min(420.0, max(18.0, slice_len))
        seed_dl = min(cont_limit, time.time() + slice_len)
        try:
            hnew, c5new, _ = continuous_pair_optimize(hseed, None, best_n, seed_dl, rng,
                                                      **params_for(best_n))
        except Exception:
            continue
        if c5new < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_c5 = c5new
            best_h = hnew
            best_n = best_n

    if time.time() + 25.0 < cont_limit:
        try:
            hnew, c5new, _ = continuous_pair_optimize(best_h, None, best_n, cont_limit, rng,
                                                      **params_for(best_n))
        except Exception:
            hnew, c5new = None, None
        if c5new is not None and c5new < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_c5 = c5new
            best_h = hnew
            best_n = best_n

    if reserve_multi > 0.0 and time.time() + 90.0 < deadline - reserve_final:
        chosen = []
        for nn in (240, 320, 256, 192, 384):
            if nn != best_n and nn % 2 == 0 and 120 <= nn <= 512:
                chosen.append(nn)
            if len(chosen) == 2:
                break
        if len(chosen) == 0:
            nn2 = best_n + 80
            if nn2 > 512:
                nn2 = 240
            if nn2 % 2 != 0:
                nn2 += 1
            if 120 <= nn2 <= 512 and nn2 != best_n:
                chosen.append(nn2)

        mult_limit = deadline - reserve_final
        for idxm, nn in enumerate(chosen):
            if time.time() + 50.0 >= mult_limit:
                break
            rem = mult_limit - time.time()
            left = max(1, len(chosen) - idxm)
            slot_dl = min(mult_limit, time.time() + max(45.0, rem / float(left)))
            try:
                h2 = project_feasible(interp_to(best_h, best_n, nn), nn)
                c2 = correlation_full(h2)
                c52 = float(np.max(c2)) * (2.0 / float(nn))
            except Exception:
                continue
            if c52 < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                best_c5 = c52
                best_h = h2
                best_n = nn

            try:
                b2 = threshold_balance(h2, nn)
            except Exception:
                b2 = random_binary(nn, rng)

            left = slot_dl - time.time()
            if left > 25.0:
                dl1 = min(slot_dl, time.time() + max(20.0, 0.55 * left))
                try:
                    hnew, c5new, _ = continuous_pair_optimize(h2, None, nn, dl1, rng, **params_for(nn))
                except Exception:
                    hnew, c5new = None, None
                if c5new is not None and c5new < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                    best_c5 = c5new
                    best_h = hnew
                    best_n = nn

            if time.time() + 20.0 < slot_dl:
                dl2 = min(slot_dl, time.time() + max(15.0, slot_dl - time.time()))
                try:
                    hnew, c5new, _ = continuous_pair_optimize(b2, None, nn, dl2, rng, **params_for(nn))
                except Exception:
                    hnew, c5new = None, None
                if c5new is not None and c5new < best_c5 - 1e-12:
                    best_c5 = c5new
                    best_h = hnew
                    best_n = nn

    if time.time() + 25.0 < deadline - reserve_final:
        try:
            hnew, c5new, _ = continuous_pair_optimize(best_h, None, best_n,
                                                      deadline - reserve_final, rng,
                                                      **params_for(best_n))
        except Exception:
            hnew, c5new = None, None
        if c5new is not None and c5new < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_c5 = c5new
            best_h = hnew
            best_n = best_n

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    final_c = correlation_full(best_h)
    final_c5 = float(np.max(final_c)) * (2.0 / float(best_n))

    try:
        ec = evaluate_erdos_solution(best_h)
        if ec is not None and ec < final_c5:
            final_c5 = float(ec)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_h, final_c5, best_n
```
